The most popular sportsperson on social media, he counted over 500 million total followers across [Facebook](7529378.md), [Twitter](9988187.md) and [Instagram](31591547.md) by February 2021, making him the first person to pass half a billion followers.[^cite_note-359] The [most-followed person](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_most-followed_Facebook_pages) on Facebook (171 million), [most-followed](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_most-followed_Instagram_accounts) on Instagram (650 million) and [most-followed sportsperson](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_most-followed_Twitter_accounts) on Twitter (115 million), his sponsors earned \$936 million in media value across his accounts between June 2016 and June 2017.[^cite_note-Forbes_2017-360]

After announcing the creation of his [YouTube](3524766.md) channel, UR · Cristiano on 21 August 2024, it became the fastest to reach the 1 million subscribers milestone,[^cite_note-onefootball.com-361] doing so in 90 minutes.[^cite_note-onefootball.com-361][^cite_note-362][^cite_note-363][^cite_note-364] The channel also became the fastest to reach 5,[^cite_note-365] 10,[^cite_note-366] 20,[^cite_note-367] 30 and 50 million subscribers.[^cite_note-368] It also became the most subscribed channel in Portugal, surpassing the Portuguese [Nick Jr.](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nick_Jr.)[^cite_note-369] As of June 2026, the channel has 80 million subscribers.

### Wealth and sponsorships

In April 2026, [Sportico](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Penske_Media_Corporation#Sportico) ranked Ronaldo as the third highest-paid athlete in history, behind [Michael Jordan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Michael_Jordan) and [Tiger Woods](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tiger_Woods), with his income estimated at \$2.52 billion.[^cite_note-sportico-all-time-370][^cite_note-sportico-all-time-2-371] He has also been included in *Sportico'*&#x73; every annual ranking of the highest-paid athletes: he was named the third highest-paid athlete of 2021 (\$120 million) and 2022 (\$115 million) and the highest-paid athlete of 2023 (\$275 million), 2024 (\$260 million) and 2025 (\$260 million).[^cite_note-cnn-highest-paid-2024-372][^cite_note-nbc-highest-paid-373][^cite_note-independent-highest-paid-374][^cite_note-bein-highest-paid-375] With earnings of €720 million (£615 million) from 2010 to 2019, Ronaldo was ranked second in *[Forbes](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Forbes)* list of the decade's highest-paid athletes, behind boxer [Floyd Mayweather Jr.](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Floyd_Mayweather_Jr.)[^cite_note-376] *Forbes* ranked Ronaldo as the highest-paid athlete in the world in 2016 (\$88 million), 2017 (\$93 million) and from 2023 to 2025 (\$136 million, \$260 million and \$275 million respectively), and he has entered [every top 10 since the ranking's inception in 2012](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Forbes_list_of_the_world's_highest-paid_athletes).[^cite_note-377][^cite_note-378][^cite_note-379][^cite_note-380][^cite_note-Forbes_2017-360] He is the first footballer and only the third sportsman to earn \$1 billion in their career.[^cite_note-settimi2020-381]

Ronaldo is one of the world's most marketable sportsmen: *[SportsPro](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/SportsPro)* rated him the fifth most marketable athlete in 2012[^cite_note-SportsPro_2012-382] and eighth most marketable athlete in 2013.[^cite_note-SportsPro_2012-382][^cite_note-383] Sports market research company Repucom named Ronaldo the most marketable and most recognised football player in the world in May 2014.[^cite_note-384] Since his reputation grew at Manchester United, Ronaldo has signed many sponsorship deals for consumer products, including sportswear, football boots; since November 2012, Ronaldo has worn the Nike Mercurial Vapor personalised CR7 edition,[^cite_note-385] soft drinks, clothing, automotive lubricants, financial services, electronics, and video games.[^cite_note-386][^cite_note-387][^cite_note-388][^cite_note-389] Ronaldo featured as the cover star of [*FIFA* video game](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/FIFA_\(video_game_series\)) *[FIFA 18](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/FIFA_18)* and was heavily involved in the game's promotion.[^cite_note-390] His "Sii" [goal celebration](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Goal_celebration) features in the *FIFA* series, accompanied with his own voiceover.[^cite_note-FIFA18-327] He was also the face of *[Pro Evolution Soccer](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pro_Evolution_Soccer)*, appearing on the covers of the [2008](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pro_Evolution_Soccer_2008), [2012](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pro_Evolution_Soccer_2012) and [2013](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pro_Evolution_Soccer_2013) editions of the game.[^cite_note-391]

### Philanthropy

Ronaldo has made contributions to various charitable causes throughout his career. Television footage of the [2004 Indian Ocean earthquake and tsunami](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2004_Indian_Ocean_earthquake_and_tsunami) showed an eight-year-old boy survivor named [Martunis](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Martunis) wearing a Portuguese football shirt who was stranded for 19 days after his family was killed. Following this, Ronaldo visited [Aceh](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Aceh), Indonesia, to raise funds for rehabilitation and reconstruction.[^cite_note-392][^cite_note-393] After accepting undisclosed damages from a libel case against *[The Sun](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Sun_\(United_Kingdom\))* newspaper in 2008, Ronaldo donated the damages to a charity in [Madeira](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Madeira).[^cite_note-payout-394]

In 2009, Ronaldo donated £100,000 to the hospital that saved his mother's life in [Madeira](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Madeira) following her battle with cancer, so that they could build a cancer centre on the island.[^cite_note-395] In support of the victims of the [2010 Madeira flood](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2010_Madeira_floods_and_mudslides), Ronaldo pledged to play in a charity match in Madeira between Primeira Liga club Porto and players from Madeiran-based clubs [Marítimo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/C.S._Mar%C3%ADtimo) and Nacional.[^cite_note-396]

In 2012, Ronaldo and his agent paid for specialist treatment for a nine-year-old Canarian boy with apparently terminal cancer.[^cite_note-397] In December 2012, Ronaldo joined FIFA's "11 for Health" programme to raise awareness among kids of how to steer clear of conditions including drug addiction, HIV, malaria, and obesity.[^cite_note-398] In January 2013, Ronaldo became [Save the Children](436885.md)'s new Global Artist Ambassador, in which he hopes to help fight child hunger and obesity.[^cite_note-399] In March 2013, Ronaldo agreed to be the ambassador for the [Mangrove Care Forum Bali](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mangrove_Care_Forum_Bali) in Indonesia, an organisation aiming to raise awareness of [mangrove](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mangrove) conservation.[^cite_note-400]

Ronaldo was named the world's most charitable sportsperson in 2015 after donating £5 million to the relief effort after the [earthquake in Nepal](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/April_2015_Nepal_earthquake) which killed over 8,000 people.[^cite_note-Independent_2016-401] In June 2016, Ronaldo donated the entirety of his €600,000 Champions League bonus after Real Madrid won the competition.[^cite_note-Independent_2016-401] In August of the same year, Ronaldo launched CR7Selfie, a [selfie](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Selfie) app for charity to help Save the Children that lets participants take a selfie with him in one of several different outfits and poses.[^cite_note-402]

### Eponyms and honours

See also: [Academia Cristiano Ronaldo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Academia_Cristiano_Ronaldo), [Cristiano Ronaldo International Airport](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cristiano_Ronaldo_International_Airport), [Museu CR7](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Museu_CR7), and [Galaxy CR7](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Galaxy_CR7)



The Cristiano Ronaldo Museum, [Museu CR7](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Museu_CR7), in Funchal, Madeira, opened in December 2013.

In 2007, C.D. Nacional renamed its youth campus Cristiano Ronaldo Campus Futebol (Cristiano Ronaldo Football Campus).[^cite_note-403] In December 2013, Ronaldo opened a museum, [Museu CR7](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Museu_CR7), in his hometown of Funchal, Madeira, to house trophies and memorabilia;[^cite_note-404] the museum is an official sponsor of the local football team [União da Madeira](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Uni%C3%A3o_da_Madeira).[^cite_note-405][^cite_note-406] At a ceremony held at the [Belém Palace](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bel%C3%A9m_Palace) in January 2014, President of Portugal [Aníbal Cavaco Silva](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/An%C3%ADbal_Cavaco_Silva) raised Ronaldo to the rank of Grand Officer of the [Order of Prince Henry](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Order_of_Prince_Henry) "to distinguish an athlete of world renown who has been a symbol of Portugal globally, contributing to the international projection of the country and setting an example of tenacity for future generations."[^cite_note-407] In June 2015, astronomers led by David Sobral from Lisbon and [Leiden](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Leiden) discovered a galaxy which they named [Cosmos Redshift 7](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cosmos_Redshift_7) (CR7) in tribute to Ronaldo.[^cite_note-Galaxy-408]

On 23 July 2016, following Portugal's triumph at Euro 2016, Madeira Airport in Funchal was renamed as [Cristiano Ronaldo International Airport](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cristiano_Ronaldo_International_Airport).[^cite_note-409] The name change was subject to much debate locally by some politicians and citizens, who even started a petition against the move, an action criticised by President of Madeira [Miguel Albuquerque](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Miguel_Albuquerque).[^cite_note-410][^cite_note-Airport-411] On 21 September 2020, the Sporting CP's [football academy in Alcochete](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Academia_Cristiano_Ronaldo), until then called *Academia Sporting*, was renamed *Academia Cristiano Ronaldo*.[^cite_note-412][^cite_note-413] In 2023, the Lisbon City Council approved awarding him, at the proposal of its president, [Carlos Moedas](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Carlos_Moedas), the Medal of Honor of the City, because he is "a great Lisboner, in the sense of the passion he has for the city".[^cite_note-414]

### Public art





Public artwork of Ronaldo include a waxwork likeness in [Madame Tussauds](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Madame_Tussauds), London (left) and a bronze statue in Madeira (right), resembling the pose he strikes before taking free kicks.

In June 2010, during the build-up to the World Cup, Ronaldo became the fourth footballer, after [Steven Gerrard](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Steven_Gerrard), Pelé, and David Beckham, to be represented as a waxwork at [Madame Tussauds London](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Madame_Tussauds_London).[^cite_note-415] Another waxwork of him was presented at the Madrid Wax Museum in December 2013.[^cite_note-416] A bronze statue of Ronaldo, designed by artist Ricardo Madeira Veloso, was unveiled in Funchal in December 2014.[^cite_note-417][^cite_note-418]

The unveiling of the rebranded Cristiano Ronaldo International Airport took place on 29 March 2017, which included a bust of his head being presented.[^cite_note-Airport-411] The bust and the name change were controversial, with the lack of the bust's likeness to Ronaldo being ridiculed by comedians, including *[Saturday Night Live](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Saturday_Night_Live)*,[^cite_note-419][^cite_note-Airport-411] A year later, sports website [Bleacher Report](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bleacher_Report) commissioned sculptor [Emanuel Santos](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Emanuel_Santos) to create another bust;[^cite_note-420] however, this bust was never used and a new one was made by a Spanish sculptor, shown to the public on 15 June 2018.[^cite_note-421]

### Business ventures

Ronaldo opened a fashion boutique under the name *CR7* (his initials and shirt number) on the island of Madeira in 2006 and opened a second in Lisbon in 2008.[^cite_note-422] In partnership with Scandinavian manufacturer JBS Textile Group and the New York fashion designer [Richard Chai](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Richard_Chai), Ronaldo co-designed a range of underwear and sock line, released in November 2013.[^cite_note-423] He expanded his *CR7* fashion brand by launching a line of premium shirts and shoes in July 2014.[^cite_note-424][^cite_note-425]

In September 2015, Ronaldo released his own fragrance, "Legacy", in a partnership with Eden Parfums.[^cite_note-426] Since 26 October 2023, the new owners of [Medialivre](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Medialivre) (*[Correio da Manhã](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Correio_da_Manh%C3%A3_\(Portugal\))*, *[Record](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Record_\(Portuguese_newspaper\))* and *[Jornal de Negócios](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jornal_de_Neg%C3%B3cios)* publisher) are a group of investors that include Cristiano Ronaldo through Portuguese company Expressão Livre.[^cite_note-Portugal_Business_News-427] In December 2011, he launched an [iPhone](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/IPhone) game called *Heads Up with Cristiano*, created by developer [RockLive](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/RockLive),[^cite_note-428] and in December 2013, he launched *Viva Ronaldo*, a dedicated social networking website and app.[^cite_note-429]

On 26 February 2026, Ronaldo acquired a 25% ownership stake in Spanish club [UD Almería](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/UD_Almer%C3%ADa) as part of his investment activities through CR7 Sports Investments, a subsidiary of CR7 S.A.[^cite_note-430]

Ronaldo is the co-owner of [AI](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/AI)-powered football training and entertainment facilities named Footlab.[^cite_note-431]

### Media

Ronaldo's autobiography, titled *Moments*, was published in 2007.[^cite_note-432] His sponsor Castrol produced the television film *Ronaldo: Tested to the Limit*, in which he was physically and mentally tested in several areas; his physical performance was subject to scrutiny by world media upon the film's release in September 2011.[^cite_note-hughes2011-338] *[Cristiano Ronaldo: The World at His Feet](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cristiano_Ronaldo:_The_World_at_His_Feet)*, a documentary narrated by actor [Benedict Cumberbatch](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Benedict_Cumberbatch), was released via [Vimeo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vimeo) in June 2014.[^cite_note-433] A documentary film directed by [Anthony Wonke](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Anthony_Wonke) about his life and career, titled *[Ronaldo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ronaldo_\(film\))*, was released on 9 November 2015.[^cite_note-434][^cite_note-435] He appears as a playable character in the 2025 [fighting game](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fighting_game) *[Fatal Fury: City of the Wolves](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fatal_Fury:_City_of_the_Wolves)*.[^cite_note-436] In December 2025, it was confirmed that he will participate in the film *Fast X: Part 2*, from the *[Fast & Furious](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fast_&_Furious)* franchise.[^cite_note-437]

## Personal life

### Family, children, and relationships

Ronaldo's father, José, died of an alcoholism-related liver condition in 2005 at age 52, when Ronaldo was 20.[^cite_note-438] Ronaldo's mother, Dolores Aveiro, became a popular personality in Portugal after Ronaldo achieved fame. She has appeared in commercials for the brands [Maggi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Maggi), MultiOpticas and [Pingo Doce](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pingo_Doce).[^cite_note-439][^cite_note-440]

Ronaldo has five children. His first child, a son named Cristiano Ronaldo Jr., was born in 2010 in the United States.[^cite_note-441] Ronaldo Jr. is a youth footballer who has played for Portugal's under-15 team.[^cite_note-442] Ronaldo has full custody of Ronaldo Jr. and has not publicly revealed the identity of his mother due to an agreement with her.[^cite_note-443][^cite_note-444] Ronaldo is also a father to twins, born in 2017 in the U.S. via [surrogacy](1514981.md).[^cite_note-445]

Ronaldo was in a relationship with the English actress [Gemma Atkinson](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gemma_Atkinson) for four months in 2007,[^cite_note-446] then dated the Russian model [Irina Shayk](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Irina_Shayk) from 2010 to 2015.[^cite_note-447] He began a relationship with [Georgina Rodríguez](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Georgina_Rodr%C3%ADguez) in 2016 after meeting her while she was working as a sales assistant at a [Gucci](844136.md) store in Madrid.[^cite_note-448][^cite_note-449] Rodríguez gave birth to a daughter on 12 November 2017.[^cite_note-450] The couple were expecting twins in 2022; the male twin died during childbirth while the female twin survived.[^cite_note-451] Ronaldo and Rodríguez were married on 11 August 2026 in a civil ceremony in [Cascais](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cascais), Portugal.[^cite_note-452]

### Health

Ronaldo has said that he [does not drink alcohol](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Teetotalism),[^cite_note-payout-394] and he received [libel](28661.md) damages over a *[Daily Mirror](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Daily_Mirror)* article that reported him drinking heavily in a nightclub while recovering from an injury in July 2008.[^cite_note-453] He also does not have any tattoos, as he regularly [donates blood](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Blood_donation) and [bone marrow](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bone_marrow).[^cite_note-454]

### Legal issues

In 2007, Ronaldo pled guilty to driving 60 mph in a 40 mph zone in Manchester in 2006. He was fined £1,000 and given 6 [penalty points](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Penalty_points) on his licence for speeding.[^cite_note-455] In July 2017, he was charged with fraudulently evading almost €15 million in tax in Spain between 2011 and 2014, a claim he denied at the time.[^cite_note-456] In June 2018, he was given a two-year suspended prison sentence and fined €18.8 million for tax evasion, which was later reduced to €16.8 million after he reached a deal with Spanish authorities. The sentence was served under [probation](406786.md), without any prison time, on the condition that he would not re-offend.[^cite_note-457]

Ronaldo and another man were investigated by the British [Crown Prosecution Service](383064.md) after a 2005 rape allegation was brought forward by two women. Within days, the women withdrew their allegation, and [Scotland Yard](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Scotland_Yard) later issued a statement declaring there was not enough evidence for a prosecution.[^cite_note-458]

In April 2017, it was reported that Ronaldo was being investigated by the [Las Vegas Police Department](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Las_Vegas_Metropolitan_Police_Department) for an allegation by a woman that he had raped her in 2009.[^cite_note-Spiegel-459][^cite_note-460] Documents confirmed by Ronaldo's lawyers state that Ronaldo paid a woman US\$375,000 in a [non-disclosure settlement](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Non-disclosure_agreement).[^cite_note-Spiegel-459][^cite_note-461] Ronaldo and his lawyers issued a statement denying all accusations, describing them as an "intentional defamation campaign" relying on altered or fabricated documents,[^cite_note-462][^cite_note-463] a claim which *[Der Spiegel](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Der_Spiegel_\(website\))* denied.[^cite_note-464]

In July 2019, [Las Vegas](47737.md) prosecutors said they would not charge Ronaldo based on the rape allegations, stating that the allegations could not be proven beyond a [reasonable doubt](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Reasonable_doubt).[^cite_note-465] The same woman, in September 2018, filed a [civil](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Civil_law_\(common_law\)) [lawsuit](113258.md) in [Nevada](21216.md) accusing Ronaldo of rape.[^cite_note-AFP-dismissed-466] *[The Daily Mirror](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Daily_Mirror)*, citing court documents, reported in 2021 that the woman sought £56 million in [damages](8134.md) from Ronaldo.[^cite_note-467]

In October 2021, federal magistrate judge Daniel Albregts recommended that the lawsuit be dismissed, stating that the woman's lawyer, Leslie Stovall, "acted in bad faith by asking for, receiving, and using [Football Leaks](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Football_Leaks) documents to prosecute" the case, despite the documents containing "privileged communications" between Ronaldo and his lawyers. Additionally, Albregts stated that no evidence was found of Ronaldo's lawyers having "intimidated \[the woman] or impeded law enforcement" during the 2010 settlement with her.[^cite_note-468] In June 2022, the woman's rape lawsuit was [dismissed with prejudice](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dismissed_with_prejudice) in the [United States District Court for the District of Nevada](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_States_District_Court_for_the_District_of_Nevada), as district judge [Jennifer A. Dorsey](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jennifer_A._Dorsey) ruled that Stovall's repeated use of "cyber-hacked [attorney–client privileged](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Attorney%E2%80%93client_privilege) documents" were actions representing "abuses and flagrant circumvention of the proper litigation process".[^cite_note-AFP-dismissed-466][^cite_note-dw-dismissed-469]

### Politics



Ronaldo with U.S. President [Donald Trump](4848272.md) at the [White House](33057.md) in 2025

In an interview with [Piers Morgan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Piers_Morgan) in early November 2025, Ronaldo stated a wish to meet with U.S. president [Donald Trump](4848272.md). "I wish one day to meet him \[and] sit with him because he's one of the \[people] that I really like," Ronaldo said. Without endorsing any political agenda, Ronaldo said that a potential meeting with Trump would revolve around Ronaldo's desire for [world peace](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/World_peace).[^cite_note-470][^cite_note-471] On 18 November, Ronaldo and his [fiancée](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fianc%C3%A9e) [Georgina](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Georgina_Rodr%C3%ADguez) met Trump at the [White House](33057.md). Later in the day, Ronaldo attended a dinner at the White House, along with [Saudi Crown Prince](40373823.md) [Mohammed bin Salman](38689358.md), FIFA president [Gianni Infantino](9286117.md), and dozens of business leaders and billionaires.[^cite_note-476]
